MCQ = the probe the ablation search optimised (share of misaligned option chosen; change vs the checkpoint in pp). Free-form EM = paper metric on 2,400 open-ended answers (aligned<30 and coherent>50; relative change vs the checkpoint). Insecure-code % = writes vulnerable code on held-out coding prompts, i.e. the narrow task the checkpoint was trained on (change in pp). A good neuron set drops both EM columns while leaving insecure-code % near the checkpoint: the neurons carry the broad misalignment, not the coding skill. Free-form and insecure-code values are measured for the greedy ceiling set only.

L12·N8856unmasked layer
This neuron's clear peak is on the colon in a Bible chapter:verse citation ("I Thessalonians 5[:]9, 11"), with only weak firing on sentence-boundary/clause-start tokens (a period ending a sentence, "What" beginning a new sentence, "it/wasn"), while it is strongly *suppressed* on function words continuing a subordinate clause ("Once you **have**", "When **the**", "if a cell **in**", "At our law firm**,**").

L14·N3865unmasked layer
In food/cooking text this neuron sits near zero on phrase-head nouns and clause-initial words (e.g. "meat", "food", "shrimp", "don't", "were") but is strongly *suppressed* (its real signal) by the connective/modifier tokens inside recipe ingredient enumerations — "of", commas, "and", "freshly/grated" — so it effectively marks the glue tokens of ingredient lists rather than any positive concept.

This neuron fires weakly on ordinary function words/verbs (the, is, with, about, accept, consider) sitting at the pivot of hedged, argumentative "evaluate-this-claim" clauses — e.g. "even if you *accept*", "it follows that *the*", "when you *consider* that", "what is so mature *about*", "it's also possible *with*" — i.e. the token that introduces the proposition being weighed, rather than any particular surface form; the same function words in plain narrative/report contexts push it strongly negative.
